Task10 Core4: Ruling:34 Approve the exact caller fact/evidence seam in physical-core-task&#45;4-caller-evidence-contract.md (proposal SHA256 f86dfabbd1edd66ee12b8ce8d44faaa4e451aa288d8b9e0bd9b7590ac62a454f). Why: R25 outer evidence arrays only required presence and could retain A=80 with original area=0 or another unit identity. Use a separate fact-only caller authority record {building_realization_id,unit_inventory_id,units:[{unit_id,physical_id,source_service_id,source_beneficiary_id,programme_ids,water_authority_programme_id,floor_area_m2}]} with ordered units; no evidence/inventory-hash self-cycle. Existing FieldEvidence only: each indexed unit needs one positive exact original m2 area claim /units/i/floor_area_m2 plus eight exact identity/choice locator claims for building/inventory/unit/draw/SID/beneficiary/ordered programme IDs/chosen authority, original_unit null for identities. Exact values/types/order, no boolean numeric/coercion; mandatory claims share one primary nonempty caller source_path/file SHA per realization, separate from inventory payload SHA. Reject missing/misplaced/duplicate/contradictory mandatory facts and inconsistent provenance before qualification/execution/RNG; extra supporting provenance cannot replace/contradict mandatory claims. No runtime file read, external authentication, SourceObject invention, new wire, free-text conversion parser or c8 geometry claim. Initial format accepts normalized original m2 authority only; other original units require a separately normalized caller fact record with raw supporting provenance retained. R33 static qualification pool remains independent of caller counts/subsets/order. Task6 reuses the pure validator on retained declarations offline without lookup/repair; Task7 preserves pre-RNG priority. Cost if wrong: strict format rejects otherwise valid caller provenance until normalized, while weak matching could permit contradictory area/identity or imply external/native authentication. Guards: precise contract, genuine ten-variant RED/GREEN, strict type/locator/unit/provenance checks, focused independent fix rereview and later incoming/public integration. This supersedes presence-only evidence interpretation, not caller A or original source semantics. Rulings34/deferred12.
